- **differential**: Use when a change claims behaviour is unchanged and a runnable reference version exists, such as the commit before a refactor or the implementation a rewrite replaces, when the user asks or as a step inside /hub. Not for changes meant to alter behaviour throughout, or where only one side can run. Runs two implementations against the same inputs and compares, with the accepted differences declared before the run. The refactor safety net, and the way to check a rewrite against the thing it replaces. ([NaiKiDEV/claude-plugins](https://github.com/NaiKiDEV/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/verify/skills/differential))

- **Differential Security Review**: Security-focused review of code changes using git diff analysis. - **differential-abundance**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Microbiome/bioSkills/differential-abundance) · ★36 · 6 copies)
- **differential-abundance-analysis**: Use when comparing taxonomic (species-level) or molecular abundances across two or more condition groups — such as 3xTg versus WT fecal shotgun metagenomic profiles — and you need log-fold changes with FDR-adjusted significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-abundance-analysis))
- **differential-abundance-analysis-metabolomics**: Use when you have a group-labelled, CSA-annotated aligned compound matrix (InChIKey/SMILES/formula meta-variables) and need per-compound differential statistics plus chemical-class enrichment (e.g. ChemRICH) between disease and control groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-abundance-analysis-metabolomics))
- **differential-abundance-benchmarking**: Use when comparing phenotype-enrichment methods for species/pathway abundance tables (16S or metagenomic), especially with limited cohort sizes, or when reproducing the VTwins-vs-conventional sensitivity/10-fold-sample-size benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-benchmarking))
- **differential-abundance-bias-correction**: Use when comparing absolute taxon abundances across experimental groups from compositional OTU/feature tables (samples × taxa counts) where unequal sampling fractions may bias relative-abundance-based tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-bias-correction))
- **differential-abundance-comparison-treatment-vs-control**: Use when HRMS full-scan LC-MS runs from treated vs. control samples need targeted re-extraction of known precursor ion m/z targets and a fold-change/direction comparison against a published result. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-abundance-comparison-treatment-vs-control))
- **differential-abundance-comparison-with-sccoda**: Use when testing whether cell type proportions shift between conditions (e.g. ischemic vs control) in a clustered single-cell AnnData object, especially when samples are unreplicated and bootstrapped calls need concordance checks against scCODA on replicate pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-abundance-comparison-with-sccoda))
- **differential-abundance-gene-list-matching**: Use when you have a mass-matched peptide identification table and a list of gene names from an upstream differential-abundance step, and need to keep only peptides attributable to proteins of the tissue-of-interest signature (e.g., HSIL(+) cluster). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-gene-list-matching))
- **differential-abundance-local-neighborhoods**: Use when comparing cell condition compositions in single-cell data with a shared low-dimensional representation but no trajectory assumed, or when computing per-cell imbalance scores for a visual diagnostic. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-abundance-local-neighborhoods))
- **differential-abundance-log2fc-computation**: Use when preprocessed LC-MS/MS feature intensity tables and MS/MS spectra must be statistically compared across sample groups (e.g. mushroom type), with per-feature log-2 fold changes extracted after spectral-similarity clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-abundance-log2fc-computation))
- **differential-abundance-metabolite-analysis**: Use when you have a metabolomics abundance matrix with disease/control (or mutant/wild-type) group labels and need to flag which named metabolites are differentially abundant — e.g. acd5 vs Col-0 chlorophyll b, D-glucose, proline decreased; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-abundance-metabolite-analysis))
- **differential-abundance-method-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating which microbiome DA method (e.g., ANCOM-BC, ANCOM, metagenomeSeq, DESeq2, edgeR) controls FDR at a nominal level (e.g., 5%) while maintaining power, via simulation across balanced/unbalanced two-group designs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-method-benchmarking))
- **differential-abundance-method-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare two or more differential abundance testing methods on simulated microbiome relative abundance data with known differential/null species labels, to decide which method has detection power. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-method-benchmarking))
- **differential-abundance-negative-binomial-testing**: Use when scRNA-Seq cells have been clustered into communities (Louvain or EVA) and you must test whether the abundance of specific cell-type/cluster labels changes between biological conditions while accounting for per-sample replicate variability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-abundance-negative-binomial-testing))
- **differential-abundance-overlap-analysis**: Use when comparing two or more normalization strategies for metabolomics LC-MS data and you need a downstream functional measure of agreement — overlap of differentially abundant metabolites — rather than QC-RSD or replicate-MAD metrics alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-abundance-overlap-analysis))
- **differential-abundance-results-plotting**: Use when you have a differential abundance results table for KEGG pathways from PICRUSt2 output plus the pathway abundance table and sample metadata, and you need a publication figure showing between-group differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-results-plotting))
- **differential-abundance-scoring-knn-graph**: Use when comparing cell abundance between patient conditions on an integrated single-cell dataset — e.g. finding which cell types are enriched in severe versus moderate COVID-19 samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-abundance-scoring-knn-graph))
- **differential-abundance-significance-testing**: Use when two isobarically-labeled conditions (e.g., TMT6plex SPS-MS3 glucose vs pyruvate) must be compared at the protein level, especially to test whether QC-score-based scan filtering shifts the set of significant proteins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-significance-testing))
- **differential-abundance-statistics-analysis**: Use when ranked abundance summary tables from aggregated metatranscriptome annotations are available and the goal is to find features with significant abundance differences between control and experimental groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-statistics-analysis))
- **differential-abundance-testing**: Use when one or more normalized metabolomics intensity matrices (rows = metabolites/features, columns = sample runs) must be compared for a binary phenotype contrast to detect DAMs and assess normalization-method agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-abundance-testing))
- **differential-abundance-testing**: Use when testing which cell types credibly change abundance between conditions (e.g., COVID-19 BALF severity groups) from per-sample cell-type counts, especially with few replicates where univariate tests give false positives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-abundance-testing))
- **differential-abundance-testing-microbiome**: Use when comparing taxa between exactly two sample groups in 16S rRNA ASV/genera count tables, benchmarking which DA tools call significant features, or deciding whether to rarefy and prevalence-filter before DA testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-testing-microbiome))
- **differential-abundance-testing-microbiome**: Use when a disease/control metagenome study requires testing which operon-derived KEGG ortholog pathways differ in abundance between groups, e.g. 74 T2D samples vs 71 healthy controls from mapped gut metagenome reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-abundance-testing-microbiome))
- **differential-abundance-tool-benchmarking**: Use when you must reproduce or verify that metagenomeSeq improves differential abundance calling on sparse, undersampled marker-gene survey data relative to default DESeq, edgeR, Metastats, and LEfSe workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/differential-abundance-tool-benchmarking))
- **differential-accessibility**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Epigenomics/atac-seq/differential-accessibility) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **differential-accessibility-analysis**: Use when comparing chromatin accessibility between a TR-perturbed condition (e.g., Runx1 cKO) and wild-type controls with replicate BAM and narrowPeak files, to produce a DAR BED set as downstream query input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-accessibility-analysis))

- **differential-accessibility-interpretation**: Use when after identifying differentially accessible peaks (via tl. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/differential-accessibility-interpretation))
- **differential-activity-rank-comparison**: Use when you need to validate a clustering-independent DAF/DEG method (singleCellHaystack) against cluster-based baselines (Wilcoxon rank-sum, t-test) by comparing rankings of the same features on shared normalized data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-activity-rank-comparison))
- **differential-activity-testing-along-1d-trajectory**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data have been ordered along a 1D pseudotime trajectory (e.g. monocle3 learn_graph/order_cells from DN to SP thymocytes) and you need genes with biased expression along that axis rather than cluster-marker DEGs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-activity-testing-along-1d-trajectory))
- **differential-alignment-significance-testing**: Use when two Roadmap-style chromatin state annotated epigenomes must be compared gene-by-gene to identify cell-type-specific genes from chromatin state sequence patterns alone (no expression data). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-alignment-significance-testing))
- **differential-analysis**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/differential-analysis) · ★745)

- **differential-analysis**: Find differentially expressed genes between conditions, regions, or cell types. Use when user wants to compare gene expression, find markers, or identify condition-specific changes. Triggers: "differential expression", "DEG", "marker genes", "compare conditions", "what genes differ", "find markers", "condition comparison", "fold change". ([cafferychen777/ChatSpatial](https://github.com/cafferychen777/ChatSpatial/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/differential-analysis))

- **differential-analysis-html-reporting**: Use when a ribosome profiling pipeline has produced a per-transcript count matrix (e.g. from htseq-count) and conditions are defined in config.yaml, and you need DESeq2-based differential calls plus a consolidated HTML report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-analysis-html-reporting))
- **differential-and-metamorphic-testing-auditor**: Use when comparing candidate execution against independent reference implementations and verifying metamorphic input-output relations. ([Harmitx7/tribunal-kit](https://github.com/Harmitx7/tribunal-kit/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/differential-and-metamorphic-testing-auditor) · ★7)
- **differential-apa-usage-analysis**: Use when you have an imputed 3' UTR RUD (relative usage of distal poly(A) site) matrix from spatially barcoded ST data with layer annotations and need to classify genes with spatial APA usage patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-apa-usage-analysis))
- **differential-apa-usage-analysis-between-anatomical-layers**: Use when a spatially barcoded ST dataset already has poly(A) site annotations and layer labels (e.g., MOB morphological layers), and you need genes with differential or spatially patterned APA usage between those layers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-apa-usage-analysis-between-anatomical-layers))
- **differential-attention**: Differential Attention (Diff-Transformer) & Noise Cancellation Engine: Dual-softmax differential attention (A_diff = A1 - lambda * A2), noise cancellation across long contexts, contrastive filtering against attention sink drift, learnable lambda balance parameters, autoregressive dual-key KV caching, and Needle-In-A-Haystack retrieval amplification. ([Sunzzx/hermes-skills-jobs](https://github.com/Sunzzx/hermes-skills-jobs/tree/HEAD/skills/research/differential-attention))
- **differential-audit**: Compare two implementations of the same thing — a port (R↔Python↔Stata), a reimplementation, a replication package, a refactor, or a new version against the old — so that agreement means something. Freeze inputs first, inventory every expected output, test the comparator itself, compare every channel (not just the headline number), and give each divergence a stable ID and a smallest witness. Use for cross-language parity, replication, upgrade/regression gates, or whenever "the numbers match" is about to license a claim. ([pedrohcgs/claude-code-my-workflow](https://github.com/pedrohcgs/claude-code-my-workflow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/differential-audit) · ★1.7k · 4 copies)
- **differential-audit**: Comparar dos implementaciones de lo mismo — un port (R↔Python↔Stata), una reimplementación, un paquete de replicación, un refactor, o una versión nueva contra la vieja — de modo que la coincidencia signifique algo. Congelar insumos primero, inventariar cada salida esperada, probar el comparador mismo, comparar cada canal (no solo la cifra principal), y dar a cada divergencia un ID estable y un testigo mínimo. Usar para paridad entre lenguajes, replicación, puertas de regresión al actualizar, o siempre que "los números coinciden" esté a punto de sostener una afirmación. ([dfmendivelso10/daniel-workflow](https://github.com/dfmendivelso10/daniel-workflow/tree/HEAD/base/.claude/skills/differential-audit))
- **differential-auditing-eval**: This evaluation probes an adversarial auditing framework where a blue team must identify a compromised model among a pair of nearly identical models. It tests the ability to detect hidden backdoors, misaligned behaviors, or injected instructions using various probing strategies under varying levels of prior knowledge. Use when the user wants to benchmark on CIFAR-10, Truthful QA, HHH, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/differential-auditing-eval) · ★3)

- **differential-calculator**: Calculate the user's differential glasses prescription for close-up work using Jake Steiner's EndMyopia methodology. Use when someone says 'calculate my differentials,' 'what glasses for my computer,' 'close-up prescription,' 'screen glasses,' or brings a prescription and working distance. This is a decision skill — it takes prescription + working distance and produces a specific differential Rx. ([cdeistopened/endmyopia-wiki](https://github.com/cdeistopened/endmyopia-wiki/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-calculator) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **differential-call-recall-assessment-across-depths**: Use when you need to test whether pairwise differential compartment calls remain stable at reduced sequencing depth, e.g., before committing to lower-depth runs or justifying shallow replicate removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-call-recall-assessment-across-depths))
- **differential-cell-abundance-analysis**: Use when you have a cell-type-by-sample abundance matrix (e.g., DCNet-inferred landscapes from bulk RNA-seq) with two labeled patient subtypes and need to find significantly differentially abundant cell types or build a subtype classifier from them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-cell-abundance-analysis))
- **differential-cell-subcluster-abundance-analysis**: Use when you have pseudotime-ordered single-cell data from trajectory inference (e.g., Monocle3) with cluster/cell-type annotations, and need to detect which cell subclusters expand or deplete significantly over pseudotime rather than at discrete time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-cell-subcluster-abundance-analysis))
- **differential-challenger**: Argue against a clinician's working diagnosis or plan instead of answering for them, testing for anchoring, premature closure, and the demographic gaps where evidence stops transferring. Use when the user says here is my differential or my working diagnosis and asks what they are missing, asks to be challenged, asks to poke holes in their reasoning, wants a devil's advocate, or wants to think a case through. Never produces a diagnosis and never ranks a differential. ([digitaljavelina/the-ai-clinicians-claude-plugins](https://github.com/digitaljavelina/the-ai-clinicians-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/differential-challenger/skills/differential-challenger))
- **differential-chromatin-accessibility-analysis-between-cell-types**: Use when you have pre-processed chromatin accessibility data (ATAC-seq or DNAse-seq) with chromVAR deviations already computed for individual cells or bulk samples across multiple cell types or conditions, and you need to identify which transcription factor motifs exhibit significant differential. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/differential-chromatin-accessibility-analysis-between-cell-types))
- **differential-chromatin-interaction-detection**: Use when you have a normalized scHi-C count matrix (rows = bin pairs, columns = cells) from two conditions and need per-bin-pair differential interaction statistics (pvalue, pvalue.adj.FDR) plus a consensus DCI set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-chromatin-interaction-detection))
- **differential-co-expression-analysis**: Use when you have single-cell UMI count data with per-cell group labels (e.g. disease status) and per-cell donor/sample labels, and need to test whether gene co-expressions within a specific cell type differ between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-co-expression-analysis))
- **differential-co-expression-network-analysis**: Use when you have matched two-condition gene expression matrices (rows = gene symbols, columns = sample IDs) plus a reference interaction network, and you need driver-node selection by network controllability rather than hub ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-co-expression-network-analysis))
- **differential-coexpression-z-testing**: Use when you have FPKM-normalized expression matrices for two physiological conditions (e.g. TCGA_BLCAtumor vs TCGA_BLCAnormal, rows=gene symbols, columns=sample ids) and need to call significant differential co-expression edges on a fixed reference network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-coexpression-z-testing))
- **differential-communication-analysis**: Use when testing whether cell-cell communication strength mediated by specific candidate eLR pairs depends on a developmental process, by comparing interaction scores between a perturbed condition (e.g., ZGA-inhibited embryos) and unperturbed time-point conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-communication-analysis))
- **differential-communication-analysis-between-conditions**: Use when two or more single-cell/single-nucleus expression matrices with cell-type annotations from distinct biological conditions (e.g., ASD vs control cortex) must be compared at the level of inferred ligand-receptor communication networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-communication-analysis-between-conditions))
- **differential-compartment-significance-testing**: Use when comparing compartmentalization across ≥2 bulk Hi-C maps (with replicates, e.g. ESC vs NPC at 100 kb) and you need per-bin significance calls for compartment changes, including within-A/within-B differences without compartment flips. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-compartment-significance-testing))
- **differential-connectomics-analysis**: Use when two single-cell RNA-seq conditions (e.g. ifnb interferon-stimulated vs. control PBMCs from SeuratData) must be compared at the ligand–receptor level, and each edge needs a magnitude-of-perturbation score that is sign-agnostic for ligand/receptor changes.
